wait = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));  const vis = e => e.offsetParent !== null || e.getClientRects().length > 0;  const setVal = (el, v) => { el.focus(); let ok = false; try { el.select(); ok = document.execCommand('insertText', false, v) && el.value === v; } catch (e) {} if (!ok) { const p = el.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ? HTMLTextAreaElement.prototype : HTMLInputElement.prototype; Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(p, 'value').set.call(el, v); el.dispatchEvent(new InputEvent('input', { bubbles: true, inputType: 'insertFromPaste', data: v })); } el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); };  const areas = () => [...document.querySelectorAll('textarea')].filter(t => vis(t) && (t.placeholder || '').startsWith('키워드 매칭'));  const kwInputOf = t => { let el = t.parentElement; while (el) { const i = el.querySelector('input[placeholder^="키워드 입력"]'); if (i) return i; el = el.parentElement; } };  const smallBtnFor = input => { let el = input.parentElement; while (el) { const b = [...el.querySelectorAll('button')].find(b => b.textContent.trim() === '추가'); if (b) return b; el = el.parentElement; } };  const bigAdd = () => [...document.querySelectorAll('button')].filter(b => vis(b) && b.textContent.replace(/[\s+]/g, '') === '추가').pop();  let u = 0, miss = [];  for (const x of UPD) {    const t = areas().find(a => a.value.startsWith(x.old) || a.value === x.c);    if (!t) { miss.push(x.old); continue; }    if (t.value !== x.c) { setVal(t, x.c); await wait(200); }    u++;  }  console.log(`✅ 기존 항목 ${u}/${UPD.length}개 내용 갱신` + (miss.length ? ` (못 찾은 ${miss.length}개: ${miss.join(' / ')})` : ''));  let n = 0;  for (const x of NEW) {    if (areas().some(a => a.value.startsWith(x.c.slice(0, 25)))) { n++; continue; }    let t = areas().find(a => a.value.trim() === '');    if (!t) { const b = bigAdd(); if (!b) { console.error('❌ [+ 추가] 버튼을 못 찾았어요.'); return; } b.click(); await wait(500); t = areas().find(a => a.value.trim() === ''); }    if (!t) { console.error('❌ 새 항목 칸을 못 찾았어요.'); return; }    const inp = kwInputOf(t);    for (const k of x.k) {      inp.focus(); setVal(inp, k); await wait(80);      inp.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Enter', code: 'Enter', keyCode: 13, which: 13, bubbles: true }));      await wait(150);      if (inp.value === k) { smallBtnFor(inp)?.click(); await wait(150); }    }    setVal(t, x.c); await wait(200);    n++; console.log(`✅ 새 항목 ${n}/${NEW.length}: ${x.k[0]}`);  }  console.log('🎉 끝! 기존 ' + u + '개 갱신, 새 항목 ' + n + '개. 확인하고 저장하세요.');})();
